# Icon System

Miuix provides a rich built-in icon system that meets the primary design needs of most applications. These icons are available in five weights: Light, Normal, Regular, Medium, and Demibold.

## Installation

The basic `miuix-ui` library includes only a small set of essential icons. The full collections live in separate artifacts, so add the one you need:

| Artifact            | Contents                        | Access               |
| ------------------- | ------------------------------- | -------------------- |
| `miuix-icons`       | Extended icons                | `MiuixIcons.*`       |
| `miuix-icons-symbol` | Variable symbol icons          | `MiuixIcons.Symbol.*` |

```kotlin
// build.gradle.kts
commonMain.dependencies {
    implementation("top.yukonga.miuix.kmp:miuix-ui:<version>")
    // Add the extended icon library
    implementation("top.yukonga.miuix.kmp:miuix-icons:<version>")
    // Add the variable Symbol icon library
    implementation("top.yukonga.miuix.kmp:miuix-icons-symbol:<version>")
}
```

## Usage

To use Miuix icons in your project, first import them correctly:

```kotlin
import androidx.compose.foundation.layout.size
import androidx.compose.runtime.Composable
import androidx.compose.ui.Modifier
import androidx.compose.ui.unit.dp
import top.yukonga.miuix.kmp.basic.Icon
import top.yukonga.miuix.kmp.icon.MiuixIcons
import top.yukonga.miuix.kmp.icon.extended.Ok
import top.yukonga.miuix.kmp.icon.extended.Settings
```

Then, you can use the icons as follows:

```kotlin
// Use the Ok icon (defaults to Regular weight)
Icon(
    imageVector = MiuixIcons.Ok,
    contentDescription = "Ok",
    modifier = Modifier.size(24.dp)
)

// Use the Settings icon
Icon(
    imageVector = MiuixIcons.Settings,
    contentDescription = "Settings",
    modifier = Modifier.size(24.dp)
)

// Use a specific weight (e.g., Light)
Icon(
    imageVector = MiuixIcons.Light.Settings,
    contentDescription = "Settings (Light)",
    modifier = Modifier.size(24.dp)
)
```

Variable symbol icons come from `top.yukonga.miuix.kmp.icon.symbol` and are reached through `MiuixIcons.Symbol`. They support any continuous `wght` value in `150f..700f` without external font files:

```kotlin
import top.yukonga.miuix.kmp.icon.symbol.Search
import top.yukonga.miuix.kmp.symbol.Symbol
import top.yukonga.miuix.kmp.symbol.SymbolWeights
import top.yukonga.miuix.kmp.symbol.rememberSymbolPainter

// Render directly with any continuous weight
Symbol(
    symbol = MiuixIcons.Symbol.Search,
    contentDescription = "Search",
    weight = SymbolWeights.Medium,
    tint = MiuixTheme.colorScheme.onBackground,
    modifier = Modifier.size(24.dp),
)

// Or pass into Miuix Icon via rememberSymbolPainter
Icon(
    painter = rememberSymbolPainter(MiuixIcons.Symbol.Search, weight = 420f),
    contentDescription = "Search",
    modifier = Modifier.size(24.dp),
)
```

## Icon Weights

Miuix icons support five weights, matching the named instances of the source font `misymbol_vf.ttf`:

- `MiuixIcons.Light`: Light weight icons (wght=250)
- `MiuixIcons.Normal`: Lighter-than-regular icons (wght=305)
- `MiuixIcons.Regular`: Regular weight icons (wght=330) - Default when accessing `MiuixIcons.IconName` directly
- `MiuixIcons.Medium`: Heavier-than-regular icons (wght=430)
- `MiuixIcons.Demibold`: Demibold weight icons (wght=500)

Variable symbols in `miuix-icons-symbol` support continuous weights from `SymbolWeights.Min` (`150f`) to `SymbolWeights.Max` (`700f`), including the five named presets in `SymbolWeights`.

## Available Icons

### Basic Icons

Basic icons include commonly used basic UI elements such as arrows and checkmarks. These icons are also used in Miuix's own components. Below is the complete list:

| Icon Name | Light | Normal | Regular | Medium | Demibold |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|

### Extended Icons

Extended icons include a wide variety of icons for different use cases. Below is the complete list:

### Symbol Icons

Variable symbol icons ship in the separate `miuix-icons-symbol` artifact and are exposed through `MiuixIcons.Symbol`. They support any continuous `wght` in `150f..700f` without external font files. Below is the complete list across the five named weight presets:

|---|---|---|---|---|---|
